== THANKSGIVINGS

* `Neh 12:27` And at the dedication of the wall of Jerusalem, they sought the Levites out of all their places to bring them to Jerusalem to keep the dedication with gladness, with *_thanksgivings_*, with singing, and with cymbals, psalteries, and harps.
* `2Co 9:12` For the administration of this service not only supplies the needs of the saints, but is also abundant by many *_thanksgivings_* to God.

_2 verses containing THANKSGIVINGS_
